Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) is a cornerstone of combinatorial optimization and arises in many practical scenarios. Although graph-based learning approaches have been explored for TSP, the question of how to exploit graph structure more effectively remains open. We present the Anisotropic Graph Diffusion Network (AGDN), a new Graph Neural Network framework designed to solve TSP. Our method tackles two central difficulties: (1) the lack of informative topological prior in fully connected TSP graphs, and (2) losing connected nodes in the optimal solution after the commonly used graph sparsification techniques. To overcome these issues, we construct a MixScore transition matrix that merges node similarity with pairwise distance, and we develop an anisotropic graph diffusion strategy that supports efficient information exchange across multiple hops. Comprehensive experiments spanning diverse instance sizes and node distributions show that AGDN consistently outperforms existing methods while keeping computation time competitive. Furthermore, AGDN generalizes well to problem sizes and distributions beyond those seen during training. The implementation is publicly available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/AGDN.

</details>


### [114] [A Human-in-the-Loop Bayesian Optimization Framework for Constraint-Aware Bioprocess Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19230)
*Samuel Stricker,Claus Wirnsperger,Alessandro Butté,Laura Helleckes,Gonzalo Guillén Gosálbez,Antonio del Rio Chanona,Mehmet Mercangöz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Pareto前沿引导采样(PFGS)框架，通过引入概率约束满足和鲁棒性优化，使专家能够在交互式多目标优化中同时权衡性能、不确定性、约束满足和输入鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人机回环贝叶斯优化框架在处理约束优化和鲁棒优化方面存在局限性，无法同时展示性能预测、模型不确定性、约束满足概率和输入鲁棒性之间的权衡关系，限制了领域专家在复杂工程问题中的决策能力。

Method: 1. 将约束优化纳入框架，把满足输出规格限制的后验概率作为显式Pareto目标，通过高斯过程后验分布解析计算；2. 引入鲁棒优化策略，采用蒙特卡洛采样方法估计用户定义输入扰动变化下的期望下置信性能，捕获可能实施偏差下的性能退化；3. 通过交互式仪表板的二维投影展示多维Pareto前沿，实现选择标准的迭代优化。

Result: 在八维补料分批中国仓鼠卵巢(CHO)细胞培养模拟器上验证了框架的有效性，成功识别出高性能、可行且对扰动具有弹性的操作条件。专家定义的需求为实验资源分配提供了原则性的停止准则。

Conclusion: 扩展后的PFGS框架能够系统性地支持复杂优化问题中的多目标权衡分析，通过可视化界面使专家能够根据改进的代理模型和演进的开发目标迭代优化选择标准，为人机回环贝叶斯优化在工程应用中的实用性提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This work presents an extension to Pareto Front Guided Sampling (PFGS), a Human-in-the-Loop (HitL) Bayesian Optimization (BO) framework in which Gaussian process (GP) surrogate-derived quantities are reformulated as objectives of a multi-objective optimization problem, and the resulting Pareto front is exposed to a domain expert for interactive candidate selection rather than returning a single automated recommendation. The framework is extended in two directions: constrained optimization is addressed by incorporating the posterior probability of satisfying output specification limits as an explicit Pareto objective, computed analytically from the GP posterior distribution; robust optimization is addressed by a Monte Carlo sampling strategy that estimates expected lower-confidence performance over a user-defined variability of input perturbations, capturing performance degradation under likely implementation deviations. The resulting multi-dimensional Pareto representation renders trade-offs between predicted performance, model uncertainty, probabilistic constraint satisfaction, and input robustness simultaneously visible through pairwise two-dimensional projections on an interactive dashboard, enabling selection criteria to be iteratively refined as the surrogate model improves and development objectives evolve. The framework is showcased on an eight-dimensional fed-batch Chinese Hamster Ovary (CHO) cell culture simulator demonstrating systematic identification of high-performing, feasibility-compliant, and perturbation-resilient operating conditions, and illustrating how expert-defined requirements provide a principled stopping criterion and support informed allocation of experimental resources.

</details>


### [115] [SCAN: Enhance Time Series Anomaly Detection via Multi-Scale Neighborhood-Centered Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19255)
*Xingze Zheng,Hanyin Cheng,Siyuan Wang,Yiting Hao,Peng Chen,Yuan Jun,Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCAN方法，通过多尺度聚类增强基于重构的时间序列异常检测，在表示层面约束模型重构代表性正常模式，在异常准则层面结合聚类成员概率与重构误差提供双重检测标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重构的方法是时间序列异常检测的主流范式，但存在过度泛化和欠泛化问题，两者难以平衡，影响检测性能。

Method: 引入多尺度聚类增强重构方法：(1)表示层面整合正常模式的聚类中心表示，约束模型针对代表性正常模式进行重构；(2)异常准则层面基于聚类成员概率导出异常置信度分数，与重构误差结合提供双重检测标准；(3)提取以邻域为中心的表示用于多视图聚类以提升聚类性能。

Result: 在多个来自不同应用领域的真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，SCAN达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: SCAN通过多尺度聚类有效解决了重构方法中过度泛化与欠泛化的平衡问题，显著提升了时间序列异常检测性能。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection plays a crucial role in a wide range of real-world applications. Reconstruction-based methods have become the mainstream paradigm, but they suffer from over-generalization and under-generalization problems, which are challenging to balance. To address this, we introduce multi-scale clustering to enhance reconstruction-based methods. At the representation level, we integrate the cluster center representations of normal patterns to constrain the model to target representative normal patterns for reconstruction, preventing dominance of powerful capacity and representation capability. At the anomaly criterion level, we derive anomaly confidence score based on cluster membership probability and combine it with reconstruction error, providing dual criteria for detection. Furthermore, the effectiveness of the cluster center representations and anomaly confidence score depends on the clustering performance. Accordingly, we extract neighborhood-centered representations for multi-view clustering to improve clustering performance. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets from diverse application domains demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of SCAN.

</details>


### [116] [Detecting Hidden ML Training With Zero-Overhead Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19262)
*Robi Rahman,Sabiha Tajdari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了仅基于零开销、隐私保护的NVML遥测数据进行GPU工作负载分类的抗对抗鲁棒性，在9款GPU模型上实现了98.2%的训练负载识别准确率，即使面对对抗性伪装最具挑战性的工作负载仍保持43-87%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件监控是AI算力治理方案的核心基础，但如果开发者能够绕过监控机制，这些治理方案将无法实施。因此需要评估GPU工作负载分类方法在对抗环境下的鲁棒性，验证仅依赖内容无关信号（不接触模型权重、训练数据或超参数）的监控方案是否具备实际可行性。

Method: 采用零开销、隐私保护的NVML遥测信号（仅观测计算的物理效应而不访问模型内部信息），通过5轮监控-规避迭代，在涵盖4代架构的9款GPU模型上，对20种规避策略族进行系统性评估，开发并验证了工作负载分类器。

Result: 构建的分类器在整个数据集上对训练工作负载的二元分类准确率达到98.2%；针对最具挑战性的意外工作负载，即使在对抗性伪装条件下仍能保持43-87%的识别准确率，证明了该方法对抗各类规避策略的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于NVML遥测的GPU工作负载监控机制展现出较强的对抗鲁棒性，能够在隐私保护的前提下有效识别训练工作负载，为AI算力治理提供了技术可行性支撑，表明仅依赖物理信号的内容无关监控方案具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Hardware-enabled monitoring of GPU workloads underpins many proposals for AI compute governance, but if developers can defeat monitoring mechanisms, such schemes are unworkable. We evaluate the adversarial robustness of GPU workload classification using only zero-overhead, privacy-preserving NVML telemetry: content-agnostic signals that observe physical effects of computation without accessing model weights, training data, or hyperparameters. Across 5 rounds of monitor-evader iteration, we evaluate 20 evasion strategy families on 9 GPU models spanning 4 architecture generations. We develop a classifier that achieves 98.2% binary accuracy at identifying training workloads across the whole corpus, and 43-87% accuracy against the most challenging unexpected workloads even when they are adversarially disguised.

</details>


### [117] [Structured Inference with Large Language Gibbs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19264)
*Sanghyeok Choi,Henry Gouk,Esmeralda S. Whitammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Large Language Gibbs方法，利用大语言模型的条件分布作为转移算子进行结构化概率推理，通过迭代重采样避免自回归生成的顺序偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型编码的知识可作为复杂世界变量的结构化推理基础，但以概率一致的方式访问这些知识存在困难推理问题。传统的单次自回归生成存在顺序依赖偏差，无法有效处理结构化概率推理。

Method: 提出Large Language Gibbs方案，将LLM的条件分布用作马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛(MCMC)的转移算子。不采用单次自回归生成，而是迭代地基于其他变量对单个变量进行重采样，利用LLM的下一token条件分布。

Result: 在合成分布采样、一致性推理任务和贝叶斯结构学习三个场景中验证了方法有效性。实验结果表明该方法避免了顺序依赖偏差，产生了反映所有局部条件分布折衷的平稳分布。

Conclusion: 在通过噪声LLM条件分布可访问的世界先验下，将LLM条件分布用于MCMC是单次生成进行结构化概率推理的实用替代方案。

Abstract: The knowledge encoded in large language models (LLMs) can serve as a substrate for structured reasoning over variables describing a complex world, but accessing this knowledge in a probabilistically coherent manner poses a difficult inference problem. We propose Large Language Gibbs, a scheme for structured probabilistic inference that uses conditional distributions of an LLM as transition operators. Rather than sampling structured objects through single-pass autoregressive generation, we iteratively resample individual variables conditioned on others using an LLM's next-token conditionals. This approach avoids order-dependent biases and produces a stationary distribution that reflects a compromise between all local conditionals. We apply this approach to sampling from synthetic distributions, consistent reasoning tasks, and Bayesian structure learning. The results suggest that the use of LLM conditionals in MCMC is a practical alternative to one-pass generation for structured probabilistic inference under a world prior accessible through noisy LLM conditionals.

</details>


### [118] [Risk Stratification for ICU Delirium using Pervasive Ambient Sensing Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19292)
*Jiaqing Zhang,Sabyasachi Bandyopadhyay,Miguel Contreras,Jessica Sena,Yuanfang Ren,Andrea Davidson,Ziyuan Guan,Tezcan Ozrazgat-Baslanti,Subhash Nerella,Azra Bihorac,Parisa Rashidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现ICU环境声音和光照强度可预测谵妄，其中声音是最强预测因子。卷积神经网络模型在声音数据上达到AUC=0.80，声音与光照结合可改善短期（<1周）预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谵妄是ICU常见且严重的并发症，但早期预测和预防仍具挑战性。环境因素如声音和光照可能影响谵妄发生，但在风险评估中常被忽视。研究旨在探究光强度和声压水平是否能独立预测谵妄。

Method: 在9个ICU中收集309名患者的数据，使用四种高效序列神经网络模型进行谵妄预测，涵盖10种预测窗口大小。采用Shapley Additive Explanations分析特征重要性和影响方向。

Result: 卷积模型表现最优，在声音数据和组合数据上均达到AUC=0.80。声音特征是主要预测因子，结合声音与光照可改善短期预测（<1周），组合模型在感知期后立即分配最高风险。

Conclusion: 被动环境传感（尤其是声音）可为谵妄风险评估提供临床有意义且可解释的信号，为丰富多模态ICU预测和预防策略提供实用途径。

Abstract: Delirium is a common and serious complication in the Intensive Care Unit (ICU), associated with increased morbidity, prolonged hospital stays, and higher healthcare costs. Despite its prevalence, early prediction and prevention remain challenging. Environmental factors such as ambient sound and light may influence the onset of delirium, yet they are often overlooked in risk assessments. In this study, we examined whether light intensity and sound pressure levels can independently predict delirium across multiple prediction horizons. We evaluated four efficient sequential neural network models on data collected from 9 ICUs across 309 patients to predict delirium for 10 prediction-window sizes. We reported feature importance and direction of influence using Shapley Additive Explanations analysis. The convolutional model achieved the strongest discrimination, with AUC = 0.80 on sound data and on combined data. Sound features were the dominant predictors overall. Integrating sound with light improved short-term ($<1$ week) prediction, with the combined model assigning the highest risk immediately after the sensing period. These findings suggest that passive ambient sensing, especially sound, can add a clinically meaningful, interpretable signal for delirium risk estimation and offer a practical pathway to enrich multimodal ICU prediction and prevention strategies.

</details>


### [119] [Does VLA Even Know the Basics? Measuring Commonsense and World Knowledge Retention in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19297)
*Nikita Kachaev,Andrey Moskalenko,Matvey Skripkin,Nikita Kurlaev,Daria Pugacheva,Albina Burlova,Mikhail Kolosov,Denis Shepelev,Andrey Kuznetsov,Elena Tutubalina,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev,Vlad Shakhuro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Act2Answer协议，通过动作选择的桌面场景评估VLA模型的知识保留能力，发现VLA模型在简单概念上表现良好，但在复杂语义类别上与源VLM存在较大差距，相关信号在中间层最显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通过微调预训练VLM获得，但其保留了多少常识和事实知识尚不清晰。传统知识敏感任务的失败无法区分是知识缺失还是低层控制泛化能力不足，因此需要一种能减少控制干扰的评估方法。

Method: 提出Act2Answer轻量级协议，将VLM知识基准适配为VLA评估：每个问题转化为一个简短的桌面场景片段，智能体执行单个物体放置动作来选择候选答案，从而获得减少控制干扰的动作接地成功率。同时引入分层意图探测来定位VLM骨干和动作头中的答案相关信息。

Result: 在7个VLA模型和9个VLM基线的大规模研究中发现：(1)VLA模型在简单概念上表现稳健，但在丰富语义类别上相对于源VLM存在较大差距；(2)VQA协同训练与更好的知识保留相关；(3)答案相关信号在VLA中间层达到峰值，但在上层会衰减。

Conclusion: Act2Answer协议成功实现了对VLA模型知识保留能力的有效评估，揭示了VLA模型在不同知识类别上的性能差异，为理解VLA模型的知识机制提供了重要见解，并为未来改进VLA模型的知识保留能力提供了方向。

Abstract: Embodied Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are typically obtained by fine-tuning powerful pretrained VLMs on robotics data, yet it is unclear how much commonsense and factual knowledge they retain after adaptation. Failures on knowledge-sensitive tasks are ambiguous, conflating missing knowledge with poor generalization of low-level control. We introduce Act2Answer, a lightweight protocol that adapts VLM knowledge benchmarks to VLA evaluation by requiring agents to answer through action. Each question becomes a short tabletop episode where the agent performs a single object-placement action to select among candidate answers, yielding an action-grounded success rate with reduced control confounds. We curate a test suite of such environments across diverse commonsense and world-knowledge categories and introduce layerwise intent probing to localize answer-relevant information across the VLM backbone and action head. In a large-scale study of 7 VLA models and 9 VLM baselines, we systematically rank models across categories, finding that VLAs show solid performance on simple concepts while exhibiting larger gaps on richer semantic categories relative to their source VLMs, that VQA co-training is associated with better knowledge retention, and that answer-relevant signals peak in middle VLA layers but attenuate in upper layers. Act2Answer is available at https://tttonyalpha.github.io/act2answer/.

</details>


### [120] [P-K-GCN: Physics-augmented Koopman-enhanced Graph Convolutional Network for Deep Spatiotemporal Super-resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19303)
*Xizhuo,Zhang,Zekai Wang,Fei Liu,Bing Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理增强的Koopman图卷积网络(P-K-GCN)，用于不规则几何上的时空超分辨率重建，在心脏电动力学重建任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真时空动力学模拟计算成本极高，传统数据驱动方法缺乏物理约束，而简单的物理启发学习难以处理不规则空间几何和复杂的时间演化动态。

Method: 设计了基于连续样条的图卷积网络提取空间依赖，引入Koopman算子理论将非线性动力学投影到紧致潜在空间进行线性化，并在优化目标中增加基于物理的损失函数以强制数据驱动重建遵循物理定律。

Result: 在3D心脏几何结构上从稀疏低分辨率测量重建高分辨率心脏电动力学，数值实验表明该方法相比基线模型实现了更高的精度，并通过理论分析证明物理增强和Koopman正则化能够降低Rademacher复杂度并紧化泛化界。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了不规则几何上的时空超分辨率重建，物理约束的引入显著提升了预测保真度和鲁棒性，为复杂物理系统的超分辨率重建提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity simulation of spatiotemporal dynamics is computationally prohibitive, necessitating efficient super-resolution techniques to reconstruct high-resolution data from coarse-grained inputs. Traditional data-driven methods often lack physical constraints, and simple physics-informed learning struggles with irregular spatial geometries and intricately evolving temporal dynamics. To tackle these challenges, we propose a Physics-augmented Koopman-enhanced Graph Convolutional Network (P-K-GCN) for spatiotemporal super-resolution on irregular geometries. Specifically, a continuous spline-based GCN is first designed to extract spatial dependencies directly from coarse graph, and Koopman operator theory is incorporated to project the nonlinear dynamics into a compact latent space where temporal progression is linearized. Second, we augment the optimization objective with a physics-based loss to force the data-driven reconstructions to adhere to physical laws for improving predictive fidelity and robustness. Finally, we provide a rigorous theoretical analysis, establishing that the physics augmentation and Koopman regularization mathematically guarantees a reduction in super-resolution error by diminishing Rademacher complexity and tightening generalization bounds. We evaluate our framework on reconstructing spatially high-resolution cardiac electrodynamics across a 3D heart geometry from sparse low-resolution measurements. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior accuracy compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [121] [Diffusion-Proof: Recipe for Formal Theorem Proving Beyond Auto-Regressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19315)
*Ruida Wang,Rui Pan,Pengcheng Wang,Shizhe Diao,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了首个将扩散大语言模型(dLLMs)应用于形式化定理证明的框架Diffusion-Proof，通过长程连贯的全证明生成和双向信息局部长块纠错，在ProofNet和MiniF2F基准上分别取得1.61%和6.14%的绝对提升，并成功解决了一个IMO问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型(AR LLMs)在形式化定理证明中面临长程连贯性保持困难和错误累积等固有局限，而能够通过多token块迭代去噪生成文本的扩散大语言模型(dLLMs)虽为有前景的替代方案，但在对长程连贯性要求极高的形式化数学领域的应用尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出了Diffusion-Proof框架，包含两个模型：(1) dLLM-Prover-7B：利用dLLMs的多token块生成能力进行长程连贯的策略使用，实现全证明生成；(2) dLLM-Corrector-7B：一种新颖的大型块扩散纠错模型，利用dLLMs的填充(in-filling)能力和双向信息进行局部证明纠错。

Result: 在相同数据集训练条件下，Diffusion-Proof相比AR LLM基线显著更优。在ProofNet-Test基准上取得1.61%的绝对提升，在MiniF2F-Test基准上取得6.14%的绝对提升。值得注意的是，该框架成功解决了一个更先进的DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B模型未能解决的国际数学奥林匹克(IMO)问题，展示了dLLMs在形式化定理证明中的独特优势。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统性地证明了扩散大语言模型在形式化定理证明中的有效性和优越性，验证了基于迭代去噪的多token块生成方式相比传统自回归方法在保持长程连贯性和减少错误累积方面的优势，为形式化数学推理领域开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Enhancing the formal math reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) has become a key focus in both mathematical and computer science communities in recent years. While significant progress has been made in using state-of-the-art Auto-Regressive (AR) LLMs for formal theorem proving, these models suffer from inherent limitations. Their next-token prediction generation methods may yield suboptimal performance due to the challenges of long-range coherence and the compounding of errors over long sequences. Recent advancements in diffusion LLMs (dLLMs), which generate text through iterative denoising of a multi-token block, offer a promising alternative. However, the application of dLLMs to formal mathematics, where maintaining long-range coherence is critical, remains largely understudied. To address the challenges above, we propose **Diffusion-Proof**, to the best of our knowledge, the first framework to train and apply dLLMs for formal theorem proving. Our frameworks contain training and inference methods for two models. The first one is *dLLM-Prover-7B*, which performs whole-proof writing with long-range coherent tactic usage. The second one is *dLLM-Corrector-7B*, which is a novel large block diffusion-based correction model. It leverages the in-filling capabilities of dLLMs to perform local proof correction using bi-directional information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that **Diffusion-Proof** relatively significantly outperforms the AR LLM baseline trained under the same dataset. **Diffusion-Proof** achieves an absolute improvement of **1.61%** on ProofNet-Test and **6.14%** on MiniF2F-Test benchmarks compare to the baseline. Notably, **Diffusion-Proof** successfully resolves one IMO problem that more advanced thinking model DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B could not solve, showcasing the unique advantage of dLLMs in formal theorem proving.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Mixed-Precision Communication-Avoiding SGD for Generalized Linear Models on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18463)
*Aditya Devarakonda,Irene Simó Muñoz,Giulia Guidi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于广义线性模型的混合精度通信避免随机梯度下降算法（CA-SGD），在NERSC Perlmutter A100 GPU上实现了5.1–6.8倍加速，同时保持与FP32 SGD相当的精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式随机梯度下降（SGD）受限于通信而非计算，每次迭代都需要跨进程的AllReduce操作。通信避免SGD（CA-SGD）通过将s次连续AllReduce替换为单个Gram矩阵AllReduce来分摊通信开销，但代价是增加了计算和带宽。现代GPU的矩阵硬件和低精度格式可以抵消这些开销。

Method: 研究广义线性模型在NVIDIA GPU上的混合精度CA-SGD。通过有限精度分析，将一次CA-SGD外迭代的局部舍入误差分解为九个独立的精度选择，仅通过低精度单位舍入误差依赖于硬件，使得到的配方可以跨GPU代次迁移。具体配方为：以低精度存储输入矩阵和边缘向量，从低精度输入计算Gram矩阵并进行高精度累加，以高精度通信，以高精度执行内部递推和权重更新。

Result: 在NERSC Perlmutter A100 GPU上，混合精度CA-SGD在逻辑回归、线性和泊松问题上与FP32 SGD的损失匹配度在0.5%以内。在epsilon、SUSY、HIGGS、synth和Poisson-synth数据集上相对FP32 SGD达到5.1–6.8倍加速。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合精度CA-SGD配方能够在保持精度的同时显著提升性能，证明了在现代GPU上使用混合精度技术优化分布式SGD的有效性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Distributed stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is limited by communication rather than computation, since each iteration requires an AllReduce across processes. Communication-avoiding SGD (CA-SGD) amortizes communication over $s$ iterations by replacing $s$ consecutive AllReduces with a single AllReduce of an $sb\times sb$ Gram matrix, trading more computation and bandwidth for fewer synchronization points. Modern GPUs with matrix hardware and reduced-precision formats offset this by accelerating the Gram GEMM and shrinking BF16 traffic. We study mixed-precision CA-SGD for generalized linear models on NVIDIA GPUs. Our finite-precision analysis decomposes the local rounding error of one CA-SGD outer iteration into nine independent precision choices, depending on the hardware only through its low-precision unit roundoffs, so the resulting recipes transfer in principle across GPU generations. The recipe stores the input matrix and margin vector in low precision, computes the Gram matrix from low-precision inputs with high-precision accumulation, communicates it in high precision, and performs the inner recurrence and weight updates in high precision. On NERSC Perlmutter A100 GPUs, mixed-precision CA-SGD matches FP32 SGD loss within $0.5\%$ on logistic, linear, and Poisson problems and reaches $5.1$--$6.8\times$ speedup over FP32 SGD on epsilon, SUSY, HIGGS, synth, and Poisson-synth. Our software is available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20448273

</details>


### [123] [Flexible Distributed Particle Filtering for the Internet of Things via Aggregate Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18483)
*Angela Cortecchia,Davide Domini,Giovanni Ciatto,Roberto Casadei,Danilo Pianini,Mirko Viroli*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于聚合计算（AC）的分布式粒子滤波（DPF）场式建模方法，通过将状态估计与信息传播抽象为计算场，解耦了核心滤波逻辑与协调策略，从而实现了在统一框架下对融合中心部署、鲁棒性及通信范围等关键设计维度的系统化定制，为开放异构的物联网环境提供了可适应性强的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式粒子滤波（DPF）算法虽然能够处理分布式环境下的非线性和非高斯估计问题，但大多依赖于特定的架构和通信假设，这限制了其在开放、异构部署环境（尤其是物联网IoT）中的适应性和可定制性。因此，需要一种能够解耦核心滤波逻辑与协调策略、支持系统化定制设计维度的通用框架。

Method: 本文提出了一种基于聚合计算（AC）的场式分布式粒子滤波方法。该方法的核心是将状态估计和信息传播表达为计算场，从而将核心滤波逻辑与协调和数据流策略解耦。这种抽象使得可以系统地定制关键设计维度，包括：融合中心的放置与弹性、聚合测量函数，以及信息传播的类型和范围。通过这种场式建模，在统一框架内实现了对不同DPF配置的灵活表达。

Result: 通过一系列计算机仿真实验，验证了该统一框架下可以派生出多种DPF配置。实验结果展示了不同配置在准确性、通信成本和鲁棒性之间的权衡关系，证明了该方法在工程化可适应DPF解决方案方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于聚合计算的场式DPF方法成功地将AC定位为在开放物联网环境中工程化可适应DPF解决方案的有效抽象层。该方法实现了核心逻辑与协调策略的解耦，支持系统化定制，为解决异构IoT环境下的分布式状态估计问题提供了新的思路和工具。

Abstract: State estimation from uncertain, distributed observations is central in many cyber-physical applications. While Distributed Particle Filtering (DPF) algorithms address nonlinear and non-Gaussian estimations in distributed settings, most solutions remain tied to specific architectures and communication assumptions, limiting adaptability in open, heterogeneous deployments-most notably, the Internet of Things (IoT). In this paper, we propose a field-based formulation of Distributed Particle Filtering grounded in Aggregate Computing (AC). By expressing estimation and information dissemination as computational fields, our approach decouples the core filtering logic from coordination and data-flow strategies. This enables systematic customisation of key design dimensions, including fusion-center placement and resilience, aggregated measurement functions, as well as the type and scope of information propagation. Through a set of in-silico experiments, we show how diverse DPF configurations can be derived within a unified framework, highlighting trade-offs among accuracy, communication cost, and robustness. Overall, the proposed approach positions AC as an effective abstraction layer for engineering adaptable DPF solutions in open IoT environments.

</details>


### [124] [Splaxel: Efficient Distributed Training of 3D Gaussian Splatting for Large-scale Scene Reconstruction via Pixel-level Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18588)
*Wenqi Jia,Zhewen Hu,Ying Huang,Yu Gong,Stavros Kalafatis,Yuke Wang,Wei Niu,Chengming Zhang,Ang Li,Sheng Di,Yuede Ji,Bo Fang,Miao Yin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Splaxel是一种通信高效的分布式3D高斯泼溅训练框架，通过像素级局部渲染和全局合成策略，在大规模场景下实现了最高7.6倍的加速，同时保持了高重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式3DGS训练方法存在两难困境：将场景划分为孤立区域会导致全局不一致性，而全局高斯级别交换则会引起巨大的GPU间通信开销，且随场景规模增长迅速成为迭代瓶颈。

Method: 提出Splaxel框架，核心策略是像素级局部渲染与全局合成：各GPU仅渲染局部高斯子集并交换部分像素值，而非同步高斯参数。通过几何和透射可见性预测减少像素级冗余，并采用无冲突相机视角整合提升GPU利用率。该方法在保持数学一致性的同时，使通信代价独立于场景规模。

Result: 在包含多达1.2亿个高斯的大规模数据集上评估，Splaxel相比最先进的分布式3DGS框架实现了最高7.6倍的加速，同时保持了高保真重建质量。

Conclusion: Splaxel证明了像素级交换策略是解决分布式3DGS训练可扩展性问题的有效途径，为大规模场景的高效实时重建提供了新的技术方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-fidelity and real-time 3D scene reconstruction, but scaling training to large-scale scenes requires optimizing hundreds of millions of Gaussians across multiple GPUs. Existing distributed approaches either partition scenes into isolated regions, causing global inconsistency, or rely on global Gaussian-level exchanges, which lead to substantial growth in inter-GPU communication and quickly dominate iteration time.
  We propose Splaxel, a communication-efficient distributed 3DGS training framework based on pixel-level local rendering and global composition. Instead of synchronizing Gaussians, each GPU renders its local subset and exchanges only partial pixel values, maintaining mathematical consistency while keeping communication cost stable as the scene size increases. Splaxel further reduces pixel-level redundancy through geometric and transmittance visibility prediction and improves GPU utilization via conflict-free camera-view consolidation. Evaluated on large-scale datasets with up to 120M Gaussians, Splaxel achieves up to 7.6$\times$ speedup over the state-of-the-art distributed 3DGS framework while preserving high reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [125] [ShuntServe: Cost-Efficient LLM Serving on Heterogeneous Spot GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18600)
*Seungwoo Jeong,Moohyun Song,Juhyun Park,Kyungyong Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ShuntServe是一个面向异构竞价GPU集群的成本高效LLM服务系统，通过roofline模型性能估算器和动态规划模型放置优化器实现异构GPU上的负载均衡，结合请求迁移和共享张量存储最小化中断影响，在Llama-3.1-70B和Qwen3-32B上相比基线提升1.42x和1.35x吞吐量，相比按需实例节省约31%成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务在云端部署时GPU资源成本高昂，竞价实例可节省高达90%成本但存在频繁中断和有限可用性问题。GPU竞价实例比CPU实例可用性更低且波动更大，依赖单一GPU类型的同构集群面临相关故障风险。异构集群可利用不同竞价池的互补可用性模式，但现有LLM服务系统针对同构环境设计，在异构GPU上部署时存在负载不平衡问题。

Method: 提出ShuntServe系统，包含三个核心技术：(1)基于roofline模型的分析式服务性能估算器，用于预测不同GPU配置下的性能；(2)基于动态规划的模型放置优化器，联合优化节点配置、并行化策略和层分配，以在异构GPU间最大化吞吐量；(3)容错机制结合保输出请求迁移和通过共享张量存储的并发初始化，通过将替换节点准备与持续服务重叠来最小化迁移停机时间。

Result: 在AWS异构集群（L4、A10G和L40S GPU）上对Llama-3.1-70B和Qwen3-32B进行评估。相比最先进基线，ShuntServe分别实现1.42x和1.35x的吞吐量提升。相比按需实例，离线和在线服务分别获得31.9%和31.2%的成本效率提升。

Conclusion: ShuntServe成功解决了异构竞价GPU集群上LLM服务的成本效率和容错问题，证明了异构GPU集群可有效利用竞价实例的互补可用性模式，同时通过智能模型放置和高效迁移机制实现负载均衡和低停机时间，为云环境中LLM服务的成本优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) services become widely adopted, the cost of GPU resources for serving these models in cloud environments has emerged as a critical concern. Spot instances offer up to 90% cost savings over on-demand instances, but their frequent interruptions and limited availability pose significant challenges for continuous LLM serving. GPU spot instances, in particular, exhibit lower and more volatile availability than CPU-based instances, making homogeneous clusters that depend on a single GPU type vulnerable to correlated failures. Heterogeneous clusters spanning multiple GPU types can address this by leveraging complementary availability patterns across diverse spot pools, yet existing LLM serving systems are designed for homogeneous environments and suffer from load imbalance when deployed on heterogeneous GPUs. This paper presents ShuntServe, a cost-efficient LLM serving system for heterogeneous spot GPU clusters. ShuntServe employs a roofline model-based analytical serving performance estimator and a dynamic programming-based model placement optimizer that jointly determines node configuration, parallelization strategy, and layer assignment to maximize throughput across heterogeneous GPUs. To enhance fault tolerance when using spot instances, ShuntServe combines output-preserving request migration with concurrent initialization via a shared tensor store, minimizing migration downtime by overlapping replacement node preparation with ongoing serving. Evaluation on Llama-3.1-70B and Qwen3-32B with a heterogeneous AWS cluster of L4, A10G, and L40S GPUs shows that ShuntServe achieves 1.42x and 1.35x higher throughput than state-of-the-art baselines and attains 31.9% and 31.2% cost efficiency improvements over on-demand instances for offline and online serving, respectively.

</details>


### [126] [HI-HCQC: A Tightly-Coupled Hardware Interface with High-Efficiency Communication for Hybrid Classical-Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18642)
*Shibo Liang,Junchao Wang,Zeyuan Wang,Feng Wang,Xiaoyu Li,Lei Li,FuDong Liu,Zheng Shan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了HI-HCQC，一种基于RFSoC的紧耦合混合经典-量子计算硬件接口，通过集成PCIe Gen3 x8接口解决了现有系统中以太网连接导致的高延迟和低吞吐量问题，实验证明其在降低端到端执行延迟和提升任务吞吐量方面显著优于传统控制系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合经典-量子计算需要在经典处理器和量子控制硬件之间频繁交换数据，但现有超导量子控制系统通常通过以太网等松耦合接口连接，导致通信延迟高且任务吞吐量受限。

Method: 提出HI-HCQC硬件接口，基于RFSoC平台集成高速RF-DAC、RF-ADC、可编程逻辑、嵌入式处理器、时钟同步电路和PCIe Gen3 x8接口，实现直接微波脉冲合成、量子比特读取以及主机服务器与量子测量控制单元之间的高吞吐量数据传输。

Result: 实验表明HI-HCQC支持6个控制通道和1个复用读取通道，能够稳定生成和采集微波信号，成功完成了量子比特光谱、Rabi振荡、T1测量、单次读取、随机基准测试和CZ门表征等实验；与传统控制系统相比，显著降低了代表性量子门和电路任务的端到端执行延迟并大幅提升了任务吞吐量。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，采用PCIe耦合的RFSoC控制硬件为可扩展且高效的混合经典-量子计算系统提供了实用的基础。

Abstract: Hybrid classical-quantum computing requires frequent data exchange between classical processors and quantum control hardware. However, existing superconducting quantum control systems are commonly connected through loosely coupled interfaces such as Ethernet, resulting in high communication latency and limited task throughput. To address this issue, we present HI-HCQC, an RFSoC-based hardware interface for tightly coupled hybrid classical-quantum computing. HI-HCQC integrates high-speed RF-DACs, RF-ADCs, programmable logic, embedded processors, clock synchronization circuits, and a PCIe Gen3 x8 interface, enabling direct microwave pulse synthesis, qubit readout, and high-throughput data transfer between host servers and quantum measurement-control units. Experimental results show that HI-HCQC supports six control channels and one multiplexed readout channel, achieves stable microwave generation and acquisition, and successfully performs qubit spectroscopy, Rabi oscillation, T1 measurement, single-shot readout, randomized benchmarking, and CZ-gate characterization. Compared with a conventional control system, HI-HCQC reduces end-to-end execution latency for representative quantum gate and circuit tasks and significantly improves task throughput. These results demonstrate that PCIe-coupled RFSoC control hardware provides a practical foundation for scalable and efficient hybrid classical-quantum computing systems.

</details>


### [127] [Re-Rooting-Based Fault-Tolerant One-to-All Broadcasting in Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18712)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对密集Eisenstein-Jacobi网络提出了一种轻量级容错广播方法，通过重新选择广播源节点使故障节点成为叶子节点，在不超过两倍网络直径时间内完成容错广播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的密集六边形/Eisenstein-Jacobi网络最优广播算法假设无故障运行，但故障的内部转发节点可能中断消息传播，导致无法完成完整投递。因此需要一种容错广播机制来提高广播可靠性。

Method: 提出基于重新选根的容错广播方法：将有效广播源重新定位到新源节点，使每个故障节点与该新源的距离等于网络直径，从而使故障节点在广播过程中成为叶子节点，无需转发消息。给出了单故障和双故障的源节点选择算法。

Result: 证明了对于密集Eisenstein-Jacobi网络中的任意故障节点对，存在共同的距离-直径节点可作为有效的重选源节点；源节点选择过程在网络直径上呈线性时间（即节点数的O(√N)）；广播过程在最多两倍网络直径时间内完成；给出了反例证明双故障保证不能一般性地扩展到任意三故障配置。

Conclusion: 所提方法在无需构建冗余生成树、备份路径或额外广播结构的情况下，提高了广播可靠性，为密集Eisenstein-Jacobi网络提供了高效的容错广播解决方案。

Abstract: Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi networks are degree-six algebraic interconnection topologies with regular structure, vertex symmetry, small diameter, and efficient communication algorithms. These properties make them suitable for parallel and on-chip communication systems in which collective operations such as one-to-all broadcasting are frequent. Existing optimal broadcasting algorithms for dense hexagonal/Eisenstein--Jacobi networks assume fault-free operation. However, a faulty internal forwarding node may interrupt message propagation and prevent complete delivery. This paper proposes a lightweight re-rooting-based fault-tolerant broadcasting method for dense Eisenstein--Jacobi networks. The main idea is to relocate the effective broadcast source to a new source node such that each faulty node is located at graph distance equal to the network diameter from the new source. Consequently, faulty nodes become leaf-level nodes in the broadcast process and are not required to forward the message. We present source-selection algorithms for one- and two-node failures and prove that for any pair of faulty nodes in a dense Eisenstein--Jacobi network there exists a common distance-diameter node that can serve as a valid re-rooted source. The source-selection procedure requires linear time in the network diameter. Equivalently, since $N=3t^2+3t+1$, the selection cost is $O(\sqrt{N})$ in the number of nodes. Since the standard one-to-all broadcast completes in one diameter time and the relocation phase is also bounded by one diameter, the proposed method completes in at most twice the network diameter. We also show that the two-fault guarantee does not generally extend to arbitrary three-fault configurations by giving an explicit counterexample. The proposed approach improves broadcast reliability without constructing redundant spanning trees, backup paths, or additional broadcast structures.

</details>


### [128] [Closed-Form and Constant-Time New-Source Selection for Fault-Tolerant Broadcasting in Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18714)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对密集Eisenstein-Jacobi网络中双节点故障的容错广播源选择算法，实现了常数时间复杂度的闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集Eisenstein-Jacobi网络的容错广播中，当故障节点破坏原有广播结构时需要高效恢复。虽然已有的重根方法保证了任意两个故障节点存在有效新源，但在不扫描网络或测试所有边界候选的情况下识别该新源仍是一个未解决的实际问题。

Method: 将双故障问题转化为涉及原点和差分节点的边界交集问题。将距离为网络直径t的边界划分为Eisenstein-Jacobi六边形的六条有向边。由于网络是商结构，在定义格上求解交集方程，需要在所有6×6边对上评估七个商格移位，最多产生252个代数系统。提出了两个算法：第一个算法计算故障位于0和A时的所有有效新源；第二个算法通过求解平移边对系统、验证候选并反向移位来为任意故障对选择一个有效新源。每个系统要么是至多有一个候选的非平行2×2线性系统，要么是可行候选形成整数区间的平行系统。

Result: 两个算法在固定字长运算模型下均以O(1)时间运行。通过对50万个采样故障对和4万次重根试验的计算验证确认了正确性：选择器总是返回有效新源，恢复的广播能够到达所有非故障节点。

Conclusion: 成功解决了密集Eisenstein-Jacobi网络中双节点故障情况下有效新广播源的闭式常数时间选择问题，为容错广播提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant broadcasting in dense Eisenstein--Jacobi networks requires efficient recovery when faulty nodes disrupt the original broadcast structure. A re-rooting-based method guarantees that, for any two faulty nodes, a valid new source exists at maximum graph distance from both faults. However, identifying such a source without scanning the network or testing all boundary candidates remains an open practical problem. This paper presents a closed-form, constant-time algorithm for counting and selecting a valid new source in dense Eisenstein--Jacobi networks under two node faults. The two-fault problem is reduced to a boundary-intersection problem involving the origin and a difference node. The distance-$t$ boundary, where $t$ is the network diameter, is partitioned into six directed sides of the Eisenstein--Jacobi hexagon. Since the network is a quotient structure, intersection equations are solved modulo the defining lattice, requiring evaluation of seven quotient-lattice shifts across all $6\times 6$ side pairs, yielding at most $252$ algebraic systems. The first algorithm counts all valid new sources for faults at $0$ and $A$. The second algorithm selects one valid new source for arbitrary fault pairs by solving translated side-pair systems, verifying each candidate, and shifting back. Each system is either a non-parallel $2\times 2$ linear system with at most one candidate, or a parallel system whose feasible candidates form an integer interval. Both algorithms run in $O(1)$ time under the fixed-word arithmetic model. Computational validation over $500{,}000$ sampled fault pairs and $40{,}000$ re-rooting trials confirms correctness: the selector always returns a valid new source, and the recovered broadcast reaches all non-faulty nodes.

</details>


### [129] [Closed-Form and Constant-Time New-Source Selection for Fault-Tolerant Broadcasting in Dense Gaussian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18715)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于商格代数构造的恒定时间新源选择方法，用于高斯网络中的容错广播重根，实现了5.92倍加速且不依赖网络规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有容错广播重根框架中的边界搜索源选择步骤需要扫描网络或边界，效率较低且依赖网络规模。作者希望将边界搜索替换为基于商格感知的代数构造，实现恒定时间复杂度的新源选择。

Method: 提出两个核心贡献：(1)恒定时间有效新源计数方法——将问题转化为高斯商中两个直径-k边界集的交集，通过九个商格副本上的侧配区间固定并集获得闭式解；(2)针对两故障节点的移位直接选择器——将问题转化为模运算后，对九个商格移位和十六个有符号线性系统进行评估，采用克莱姆法则和非平行系统处理，最多检查144种情况，在字RAM模型下实现O(1)选择。

Result: 在26,623个测试节点上计数零失配；50万个故障对采样产生50万个有效输出；40,000次重根广播试验全部成功。在k=200时实现5.92倍加速，且随k增大保持稳定。方法使新源选择具有代数性、有界性和网络规模无关性。

Conclusion: 通过商格感知的代数构造，成功将容错广播的新源选择从边界搜索转变为恒定时间操作，方法准确高效，性能稳定，且不依赖网络规模，为密集高斯网络的容错广播提供了理论完备且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant broadcasting in dense Gaussian networks is recovered by re-rooting the broadcast at a new source at maximum graph distance from the faulty nodes. This paper extends the re-rooting framework by replacing its boundary-search source-selection step with a quotient-lattice-aware algebraic construction. The first contribution is a constant-time counting method for valid new sources, formulated as an intersection of two diameter-$k$ boundary sets in the Gaussian quotient. The exact count is obtained by a fixed union of side-pair intervals over nine quotient-lattice copies, giving a closed-form procedure without scanning the network or boundary. The second contribution is a shifted direct selector for two arbitrary faulty nodes. Given faulty nodes $A$ and $B$, the problem is translated to $C=\operatorname{mod}_{G_k}(B-A)$, and the selector finds $P$ satisfying $d(P,0)=d(P,C)=k$. For each of nine quotient-lattice shifts, sixteen signed linear systems are checked. Nonparallel systems are solved via Cramer's rule; parallel systems are handled by interval-endpoint selection. At most $9\times16=144$ shifted sign cases are evaluated, giving $O(1)$ selection under the word-RAM model. Validation reports zero count mismatches over $26{,}623$ tested nodes, $500{,}000$ valid outputs over $500{,}000$ sampled fault pairs, and $40{,}000$ successful re-rooted broadcast trials. The shifted selector achieves a $5.92\times$ speedup over boundary search at $k=200$, remaining stable as $k$ increases. These results make new-source selection algebraic, bounded, and independent of network size.

</details>


### [130] [ReMP: Low-Downtime Runtime Model-Parallelism Reconfiguration for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18741)
*Haipeng Yuan,Kaining Zheng,Yongshu Bai,Yuchen Zhang,Yunquan Zhang,Baodong Wu,Xiang Gao,Daning Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ReMP框架，通过解耦模型并行拓扑与运行时状态、设计二维KV缓存迁移机制等技术，实现LLM推理系统的低停机在线重配置，将拓扑切换时间从分钟级缩短至秒级，大幅优于传统重启方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理系统将模型并行拓扑(TP/PP)视为静态配置，无法在运行时灵活调整，这与实际场景中动态变化的推理负载产生根本矛盾。当前SOTA系统缺乏在线重配置能力，切换配置需重启服务，导致数分钟服务中断、KV缓存丢失和巨大的重计算开销。

Method: 提出ReMP运行时模型并行重配置框架，包含三项关键技术：(1)将模型并行拓扑与运行时状态解耦，避免完整服务重构；(2)设计二维KV缓存迁移机制，在TP/PP变更后保留可复用的缓存状态；(3)实现端到端在线重配置。

Result: 实验表明，ReMP在7B至70B参数模型上可在1-7秒内完成大部分拓扑切换，相比重启方式加速数十至百倍以上；在动态负载下显著优于固定配置，在TTFT、TPOT和输出吞吐量方面表现优异。

Conclusion: ReMP成功实现了LLM推理系统的低停机在线重配置，解决了静态并行拓扑与动态负载之间的矛盾，为大规模模型部署提供了灵活高效的解决方案，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Current large language model (LLM) inference systems universally deploy ultra-large-scale models using a combination of Tensor Parallelism (TP) and Pipeline Parallelism (PP). However, existing systems treat the model parallelism topology as a static configuration that cannot be flexibly adjusted at runtime. This rigid design creates a fundamental contradiction with the dynamically changing inference workloads in real-world scenarios. State-of-the-art systems lack online reconfiguration capabilities and can only switch configurations by restarting the service, resulting in several minutes of service interruption, KV cache loss, and prohibitive recomputation overhead. To address this problem, this paper presents ReMP, a runtime model parallelism reconfiguration framework that supports low downtime. ReMP achieves dynamic adjustment through three key techniques: (1) decoupling the model parallelism topology from runtime state to avoid full service reconstruction; (2) designing a two-dimensional KV cache migration mechanism to preserve reusable cache states after TP/PP changes; and (3) implementing end-to-end online reconfiguration. Experiments demonstrate that ReMP can complete most topology switches within 1-7 seconds on models ranging from 7B to 70B parameters, achieving speedups of tens to over a hundred times compared to the restart approach. Moreover, ReMP significantly outperforms fixed configurations under dynamic workloads, delivering superior performance in terms of TTFT, TPOT, and output throughput.

</details>


### [131] [Compressed-Resident Genomics: Full-Pipeline Device-Resident GPU LZ77 Decode with Position-Invariant Random Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18900)
*Yakiv Shavidze*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持随机访问的高吞吐GPU基因组数据解压方案，在FASTQ上达到260GB/s，随机读取比samtools快6倍且索引更小，解决了大规模基因组档案存储与快速访问的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因组数据归档（如欧洲核酸档案）增长速度远超解压能力，gzip等传统压缩方式为串行计算，现有GPU解压器不支持随机访问，而CPU工具虽支持区域查找但速度受限，亟需兼顾高吞吐与随机访问的解压方案。

Method: 扩展ACEAPEX（lzbench官方收录的绝对偏移并行LZ77编解码器），实现：1）全设备驻留GPU解码流水线（熵解码与匹配解析均在GPU上）；2）位置不变随机访问与紧凑坐标索引；3）范围解码策略，解耦输出大小与显存限制；同时评估Meta DietGPU ANS作为开放熵解码方案。

Result: FASTQ解压速度达260GB/s；任意读取解码耗时0.362ms，比samtools faidx快约6倍，索引体积小6.3倍；50GB基因组范围解码速度165.7GB/s，解决了全文件解码显存不足问题；H100上DietGPU ANS解码速度592GB/s，所有结果比特完美。

Conclusion: 实现了开放、高吞吐且支持随机访问的GPU基因组解压栈，显著优于现有CPU和GPU方案，证明完全开放的高通量解压技术路线可行，为大规模基因组档案的高效存取提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Genomic archives grow faster than decompression keeps up: the European Nucleotide Archive holds tens of petabytes of fastq.gz, and gzip is fundamentally sequential. GPU decompressors (nvCOMP DEFLATE at ~50GB/s on A100) decode whole files with no random access; CPU genomic tools (CRAM, samtools) support region seeks but only at CPU speed. We extend ACEAPEX, an absolute-offset parallel LZ77 codec included in the official lzbench 2.3 release, with three contributions absent from our prior work. First, a full device-resident GPU decode pipeline (entropy and match resolution both on-device) reaching up to 260GB/s on FASTQ, closing the match-phase-only gap of the earlier paper. Second, position-invariant random access with a compact coordinate index: an arbitrary read decodes in 0.362ms, ~6x faster than warm samtools faidx, with a read-to-block index 6.3x smaller than a .fai. Third, a range-decode strategy that decouples output size from VRAM, sustaining 165.7GB/s on a 50GB genome where whole-file decode runs out of memory. All results are bit-perfect. We also measure Meta's open DietGPU ANS on H100 at 592GB/s decode, faster than the proprietary entropy stage we currently use, showing a fully open high-throughput stack is viable. Code is MIT-licensed.

</details>


### [132] [Urban Limits as Design Constraints: Identifying Suitable Locations for Distributed, Photovoltaic-Powered Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18940)
*Justin Chikhaoui,Thomas Leduc,Daniel Siret,Abdoulaye Gamatie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在城市结构、环境和社会约束下识别分布式服务器部署位置的方法，并以蒙彼利埃为例展示了在现有基础设施上部署太阳能供电分布式计算设施的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献关注边缘计算和雾计算降低延迟和能耗的技术优势，但忽视了这些基础设施在城市中的物理和社会部署位置问题，缺乏对城市约束、环境影响和公平性考量的研究。城市地区面临数字化需求增长、资源有限和社会约束的建筑环境之间日益紧张的关系，需要找到合适的解决方案。

Method: 提出基于结构、环境和社会限制识别城市分布式服务器部署位置的方法论。该方法完全依赖现有基础设施和人工化表面，结合法律框架、在建城市项目、公民咨询和科学文献，构建基于地点的可行场地类型词汇表，并通过能源、空间和定性标准进行评估。

Result: 在法国蒙彼利埃市的应用结果表明，城市约束和本地资源决定了去中心化太阳能供电数字基础设施的可行性。研究揭示了不同类型城市空间在分布式计算设施部署方面的潜力和限制，证明了本地资源和约束条件对可行性的塑造作用。

Conclusion: 领土化方法对于在城市限制内重新思考数字服务具有重要价值。该方法为可持续、公平的城市数字基础设施规划提供了新思路，强调了在城市环境中部署分布式计算基础设施时需要考虑物理、环境和社会维度的综合约束。

Abstract: Urban territories face growing tensions between increasing digital demand, limited resources, and socially constrained built environments. Although distributed computing paradigms such as edge and fog computing are widely presented as solutions for reducing latency and energy dissipation, the scientific literature largely overlooks where such infrastructures can be physically and socially deployed within cities, and typically neglects urban constraints, environmental impacts, and equity considerations.
  This paper proposes a methodology for identifying suitable urban locations for deploying distributed servers under structural, environmental, and social limits. Relying exclusively on existing infrastructures and anthropised surfaces, it combines legal frameworks, ongoing urban projects, citizen consultations, and scientific literature to construct a place-based glossary of viable site typologies, evaluated through energy, spatial, and qualitative criteria. Applied to the French city of Montpellier, our results illustrate how urban constraints and local resources shape the feasibility of decentralised, solar-powered digital infrastructures, and highlight the value of territorialised approaches for rethinking digital services within urban limits.

</details>


### [133] [LiveStack: OS Support for Cluster-Scale Full-Stack Live Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18958)
*Yiliang Wan,Haifeng Sun,Yihan Yang,Jonas Kaufmann,Antoine Kaufmann,Jialin Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了LiveStack，一种OS级集群规模全栈仿真方法，在未修改生产栈上实现了全栈保真度与仿真性能的兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集群规模全栈仿真对分布式软件栈与新兴硬件组件部署前评估至关重要，需在未修改生产栈的全栈保真度与迭代配置探索的仿真性能间取得平衡，但现有方法无法同时满足两者。

Method: 基于Linux虚拟化栈，构建四个子系统：面向仿真的调度、实时内存层级管理、仿真感知IPC及分布式仿真编排，在共享仿真时间下协调实时与建模组件，并控制共置实时主机间的干扰。

Result: LiveStack实现了集群规模全栈仿真，达成了全栈保真度与仿真性能的双重目标。

Conclusion: 该机制为仿真原生OS支持指明方向，仿真控制与编排成为OS核心职责。

Abstract: Cluster-scale full-stack simulation is essential for evaluating distributed software stacks and emerging hardware components before deployment. Such simulation must achieve both full-stack fidelity for the unmodified production stack and the simulation performance required for iterative configuration exploration. However, no existing method achieves both. We present LiveStack, an OS-level approach to cluster-scale full-stack simulation built on top of the Linux virtualization stack. LiveStack comprises four subsystems: simulation-oriented scheduling, live memory hierarchy management, simulation-aware IPC, and distributed simulation orchestration. Together, they coordinate live and modeled components under shared simulated time while controlling interference among co-located live hosts. These mechanisms point toward simulation-native OS support, where simulation control and orchestration become core OS responsibilities.

</details>


### [134] [A Composable CRDT Layer for Byzantine-Resilient Deterministic Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18966)
*Amos Brocco*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过确定性状态重构来实现拜占庭容错CRDT的方法，无需协调即可保证强最终一致性，并在Melda系统中实现了这一方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CRDT假设参与者是良性的，面对拜占庭行为时通常依赖验证或排除机制。本文旨在解决CRDT在恶意参与者存在时的容错问题，避免依赖传统的验证或排除方法。

Method: 提出确定性状态重构方法：所有被接受的更新都被纳入，但只有部分更新参与状态重构。在Melda系统中实现该方法，这是一种针对JSON文档的非侵入式增量状态CRDT。将更新传播与状态推导解耦，恶意更新要么被结构性拒绝，要么作为重构过程的输入。

Result: 形式化证明了该模型能够保证收敛性：即使存在任意更新注入、等效、遗漏或消息重排序，从相同更新集派生状态的副本也不会产生分歧。此外，认证、授权和保密性可以分层添加而不影响收敛性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过解耦更新传播与状态推导可以实现拜占庭容错，更新的一致性可由外部传播或共识机制独立处理，为CRDT的安全性和容错性提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: Conflict-free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs) ensure Strong Eventual Consistency without coordination, but typically assume benign participants and rely on validation or exclusion to handle Byzantine behavior.
  We address this problem through deterministic state reconstruction: rather than deciding which updates are admissible, all accepted updates are incorporated, while only a subset contributes to the reconstructed state. We instantiate this approach in Melda, a non-intrusive delta-state CRDT for JSON documents, and show that its reconstruction model guarantees convergence even under arbitrary update injection: adversarial updates are either structurally rejected or treated as inputs to the reconstruction process.
  We formalize this model and prove that replicas deriving state from the same set of updates cannot diverge despite equivocation, omission, or message reordering. We further show that authentication, authorization, and confidentiality can be layered without affecting convergence.
  Overall, this approach suggests that Byzantine tolerance can be achieved by decoupling update propagation from state derivation, allowing agreement on updates to be handled independently by external dissemination or consensus mechanisms.

</details>


### [135] [On the Notions of Bounded Bypass, and How to Make any Deadlock-Free MUTEX Protocol Satisfy One of Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19003)
*Rob van Glabbeek,Daniele Gorla,Myrthe Spronck*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次形式化定义了有界超越（bounded bypass），提出了两个介于无饥饿与有界超越之间的新活性属性，并证明了Bar-David算法能够将死锁自由的互斥协议升级为具有有界超越性质的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在互斥问题研究中，有界超越长期以来被用作无饥饿性质的增强，但缺乏一个令人满意的形式化定义；同时，某些MUTEX协议需要对该概念进行适当弱化才能满足要求。

Method: 首先给出了有界超越的形式化定义（纠正了Raynal先前的定义），然后提出了后门阶段有界超越和间歇性有界超越两个新概念，最后使用时序逻辑公式对各种有界超越概念进行建模并通过模型检测验证所有结论。

Result: 证明了Bar-David算法在原子寄存器设置下能将任意死锁自由的互斥协议升级为有界超越协议（界限为进程数的二次函数）；在安全和常规寄存器设置下确保间歇性有界超越（界限仍为二次函数但略有不同）。模型检测帮助发现并纠正了定义和证明中的小错误和歧义。

Conclusion: 本文为有界超越提供了严格的形式化基础，引入了两个有用的中间活性属性，并通过算法分析和模型检测验证了理论结果的正确性，对互斥协议的活性属性研究做出了重要贡献。

Abstract: In the literature on mutual exclusion, bounded bypass has been used for a long time as a strengthening of starvation-freedom, but, to the best of our knowledge, it still lacks a satisfying definition as a liveness property on its own. Moreover, we have encountered MUTEX protocols for which this notion needs to be slightly weakened in order to be met.
  To solve these issues, we first provide a formal definition of bounded bypass (that also corrects a previous definition from Raynal) and then introduce the notions of post-doorway and intermittent bounded bypass, two liveness properties that lie between starvation-freedom and bounded bypass. Essentially, intermittent bounded bypass weakens bounded bypass by ignoring the possible bypasses that may happen during the execution of a certain finite set of write operations to shared registers. Orthogonally, post-doorway bounded bypass ignores the bypasses that may happen during a finite initial phase of the lock protocol.
  Furthermore, we study an algorithm proposed by Yoah Bar-David in 1998 to enhance the liveness properties of any deadlock-free MUTEX protocol and prove that: (1) in the setting of atomic registers, this algorithm upgrades any deadlock-free mutual exclusion protocol to a bounded bypass one, with a bound that is quadratic in the number of processes; and (2) in the setting of safe and regular registers, the very same algorithm ensures the intermittent version of bounded bypass, still with a quadratic (but slightly different) bound.
  Finally, we provide logical formulae for the different notions of bounded bypass defined in this paper and use them to confirm all claims made here, by using model checking. This had a positive impact on the theoretical development of the work, since it allowed us to identify and correct small mistakes/ambiguities in definitions and proofs.

</details>


### [136] [Spotlight: Synergizing Seed Exploration and Spot GPUs for DiT RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19004)
*Ruiqi Lai,Dakai An,Wei Gao,Ju Huang,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Dmitrii Ustiugov,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Spotlight是首个利用竞价型GPU进行DiT强化学习后训练的系统，通过容忍过时模型权重的探索和快速SP组重配置，在保持图像质量的同时将训练速度提升4倍、成本降低1.4-6.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformers的强化学习后训练成本极其高昂，需要数千张高端GPU。现有降本方案存在局限：种子探索虽能提升收敛但增加关键路径计算；竞价型GPU成本低69-77%，但因DiT rollout几乎同时完成而无法流水线化，且抢占会破坏序列并行组。

Method: 提出Spotlight系统，包含三项技术：(1)基于老虎机的探索规划器，在训练时间预算内最大化奖励方差；(2)弹性序列并行，通过持久调度器和节点内权重拷贝实现SP组动态重配置；(3)抢占感知的拉取式请求调度器，平衡负载并在抢占时提交运行状态。核心发现是探索可容忍过时模型权重，且SP重配置可复用节点状态实现亚秒级恢复。

Result: 在Qwen-Image后训练任务上，Spotlight比基线快4倍达到目标验证分数，总成本降低1.4-6.4倍，在DeepSeek-OCR和Geneval数据集上（分辨率512×512和1280×1280）实现了更优的图像质量。

Conclusion: Spotlight成功解决了DiT RL后训练的高成本问题，首次实现了竞价型GPU的有效利用，证明了过时权重探索和弹性序列并行的可行性，为大规模扩散模型训练提供了经济高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) is prohibitively expensive, requiring thousands of high-end GPUs. Existing works explore two directions to reduce cost: seed exploration improves training convergence by selecting high-contrast samples, yet adds compute to the critical path; spot GPUs offer 69--77\% lower cost, yet sit idle during training because DiT rollouts finish nearly simultaneously, which prevents LLM-style pipelining of rollout with training. Spot preemptions further break Sequence Parallelism (SP) groups, fragmenting GPU topology.
  We present Spotlight, the first system that harvests spot GPUs for DiT RL post-training. Spotlight rests on two key insights we devise: (1)~we show that exploration can tolerate stale model weights because exploration that uses the model weights from the previous iteration preserves the relative ranking of random seeds, allowing exploration to run on idle spot GPUs during training. (2)~SP reconfiguration can reuse on-node state, reducing group recovery from minutes to sub-second launches. Built on these insights, Spotlight introduces three techniques: a bandit-based exploration planner that maximizes reward variance within the training time budget, elastic sequence parallelism that reconfigures SP groups on the fly via persistent schedulers and intra-node weight copying, and a preemption-aware pull-based request scheduler that balances load and commits in-flight state upon preemption. We implement Spotlight on the open-source RL platform ROLL and evaluate it on Qwen-Image post-training. Spotlight reaches the same target validation score $4\times$ faster than baselines, reducing total cost by $1.4$-$6.4\times$ while achieving superior image quality on DeepSeek-OCR and Geneval datasets with resolution $512\times512$ and $1280\times1280$.

</details>


### [137] [Pulse: Training Acceleration for Large Diffusion Models with Automatic Pipeline Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19163)
*Boran Sun,Guoyong Jiang,Lin Zhang,Chen Chen,Yuechen Tao,Zhishu Che,Jieling Yu,Shan Chang,Huaxi Gu,Fangming Liu,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出PULSE，一种面向扩散模型的自动流水线并行训练策略，通过将跳连的编码器-解码器层共置在同一设备并本地缓存跳连激活，显著减少通信开销，在通信受限硬件上最高提升2.3倍吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型主流采用UNet式编码器-解码器结构，存在长程跳跃连接。在传统流水线并行下，这些非局部依赖导致大量跳跃激活及其梯度需跨多个流水线边界传输，点对点（P2P）通信成为主导瓶颈，严重降低流水线效率。现有方法未充分考虑跳跃连接的局部性，通信开销巨大。

Method: 提出PULSE策略，以跳跃局部性为核心优化目标：1）跳连感知的动态规划划分器，在对称共置约束下平衡异构阶段负载；2）基于整数线性规划（ILP）的调度合成器，生成气泡高效波动调度；3）混合并行调优器，在内存和网络约束下选择流水线/数据并行度与微批大小。通过将跳跃连接的编解码层共置同设备并本地缓存激活，消除跳跃引发的跨设备通信。

Result: 相比最先进并行策略，PULSE将通信量减少89%，在通信受限硬件上训练吞吐量最高提升2.3倍。实验验证了方法在多种配置下的有效性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: PULSE通过将跳跃局部性作为一等优化目标，成功解决了扩散模型流水线并行中的通信瓶颈问题，实现了高效的分布式训练，为大规模扩散模型训练提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models are now a dominant approach for high-fidelity image and video generation, yet scaling their training across GPU clusters remains challenging. Unlike transformer-only architectures, diffusion backbones commonly adopt UNet-style encoder-decoder structures with heterogeneous layers and long-range skip connections. Under conventional pipeline parallelism, these non-local dependencies force large skip activations and their gradients to traverse multiple pipeline boundaries, making peer-to-peer (P2P) communication a dominant bottleneck and substantially reducing pipeline efficiency. In this paper, we present PULSE, an automatic pipeline-parallel training strategy that makes skip locality a first-class optimization objective. PULSE eliminates skip-induced communication by collocating skip-connected encoder-decoder layers on the same device and caching skip activations locally for later use in backpropagation. To realize this placement while maintaining high pipeline utilization, PULSE co-designs: (1) a skip-aware dynamic-programming partitioner that balances heterogeneous stage workloads under symmetric collocation constraints, (2) an ILP-based schedule synthesizer that generates bubble-efficient wave schedules for the resulting stage-to-device mapping, and (3) a hybrid parallelism tuner that selects pipeline/data-parallel degrees and microbatch sizes under memory and network constraints. Our extensive experiments show that the volume of communication can be reduced by 89 percent, and the training throughput can be increased by up to 2.3x on communication-bound hardware, compared with state-of-the-art parallelism strategies.

</details>


### [138] [TurboServe: Serving Streaming Video Generation Efficiently and Economically](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19271)
*Youhe Jiang,Haoxu Wang,Haotong Bao,Kai Jiang,Jianfei Chen,Jun Zhu,Fangcheng Fu,Jintao Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了TurboServe，首个面向流式视频生成负载的推理服务系统，通过联合优化会话放置和GPU资源配置，显著降低了延迟并节省了成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频生成作为新型服务负载，需要跨活跃与空闲周期保持会话状态、反复调度持续会话并在严格延迟目标下交付每个视频块。这带来了两大挑战：会话持续时间异构性（长期运行的会话导致放置决策随时间变得次优）和时间性用户需求异构性（活跃会话数量在突发与空闲期间剧烈波动）。

Method: TurboServe将服务问题建模为联合协调会话放置和GPU资源供给的在线调度问题。核心是闭环调度算法，结合了迁移感知放置控制器（跨GPU重平衡会话以降低最大块延迟）和负载驱动自动扩缩容控制器（适应负载变化提升成本效率）。系统实现了合并块处理以批处理同GPU上的并发活跃会话、GPU-CPU卸载支持会话暂停与恢复、以及基于NCCL的GPU间迁移支持在线重平衡。

Result: 在Shengshu Technology真实生产轨迹、多种模型规模及最大64块NVIDIA B300 GPU集群上的评估显示，相比基线配置，TurboServe平均降低最坏情况块延迟37.5%，总GPU运营成本平均降低37.2%。

Conclusion: TurboServe成功解决了流式视频生成场景下的核心挑战，证明了面向流式生成负载的专用系统设计的必要性和有效性，为相关应用提供了高效的服务解决方案。

Abstract: Streaming video generation is emerging as a new serving workload in which users interact with long-lived sessions that generate video progressively, chunk by chunk. Unlike offline video generation or typical LLM serving, streaming video generation must preserve session state across active and idle periods, repeatedly schedule ongoing sessions, and deliver each chunk under a tight latency target. This creates two key serving challenges in multi-user, multi-GPU environments: session duration heterogeneity, where long-running sessions make placement decisions suboptimal over time, and temporal user-demand heterogeneity, where the number of active sessions fluctuates sharply across bursts and idle periods.
  We present TurboServe, the first serving system designed specifically for streaming video generation workloads. TurboServe formulates serving as an online scheduling problem that jointly coordinates session placement and GPU provisioning. Its closed-loop scheduling algorithm combines a migration-aware placement controller, which rebalances sessions across GPUs to reduce the maximum per-chunk latency, with a load-driven autoscaling controller, which adapts the GPU budget to workload variation for improved cost efficiency. To support these decisions at runtime, TurboServe implements coalesced chunk processing for batching concurrent active sessions on the same GPU, GPU-CPU offloading for session suspension and resumption, and NCCL-based GPU-GPU migration for online rebalancing. We evaluate TurboServe on real-world production traces from Shengshu Technology across multiple model sizes and GPU clusters with up to 64 NVIDIA B300 GPUs. Compared with baseline serving configurations, TurboServe reduces worst-case per-chunk latency by 37.5% and total GPU operating cost by 37.2% on average. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/shengshu-ai/TurboServe.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Mitigating Anchoring Bias in LLM-Based Agents for Energy-Efficient 6G Autonomous Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18272)
*Hatim Chergui,Claudia Carballo González,Farhad Rezazadeh,Merouane Debbah*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM智能体的自主资源协商框架，用于6G零接触网络切片。针对智能体的锚定偏差问题，通过截断三参数Weibull分布的随机锚定策略进行去偏，结合CVaR保证SLA尾延迟，实现高达25%的节能效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型(LLM)的网络切片智能体存在固有的锚定偏差问题——智能体会僵化地坚持初始启发式提案，导致严重的网络过度配置资源，无法有效满足严格的服务等级协议(SLA)要求。这一认知偏差需要系统性地缓解，以实现高效的资源协商。

Method: 1) 提出基于截断三参数Weibull分布建模的随机锚定策略，系统性地缓解智能体的锚定偏差；
2) 将该策略与采用条件风险价值(CVaR)的突发感知数字孪生(DT)无缝集成，严格保证SLA尾延迟约束；
3) 提出并证明双模态约束规避效用定理，揭示可行协商遵循经典凸边界，而高约束场景发生由逆有理衰减包络支配的相变；
4) 使用本地部署的1B参数模型(otel-llm-1b-it)验证方法有效性。

Result: 1) 实证结果验证了双模态边界理论；
2) 认知去偏成功打破僵化的协商模式，强制智能体主动探索以安全地沿SLA边界运行；
3) 系统节能提升高达25%；
4) 轻量级1B LLM实现亚秒级推理延迟(均值0.95秒)，满足O-RAN非实时RAN智能控制器(non-RT RIC)的运行时序要求。

Conclusion: 本研究成功开发了一种自主智能体资源协商框架，通过数学上有界的随机锚定策略有效缓解LLM智能体的锚定偏差。该方法不仅理论上有保证(双模态约束规避效用定理)，而且在实践中实现了显著的能效提升(25%)和亚秒级推理延迟，证明了其与O-RAN架构的兼容性和实际部署可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents an autonomous agentic resource negotiation framework designed to enable zero-touch network slicing in 6G architectures using Large Language Model (LLM) agents. While LLMs offer powerful reasoning capabilities, we demonstrate that such agents inherently suffer from anchoring bias, rigidly adhering to initial heuristic proposals and causing severe network over-provisioning. To systematically mitigate this cognitive bias, we propose a novel randomized anchoring strategy modeled via a Truncated 3-Parameter Weibull distribution. This mathematically bounded approach seamlessly integrates with burst-aware Digital Twins (DTs) employing Conditional Value at Risk (CVaR) to rigorously guarantee strict Service Level Agreement (SLA) tail-latencies. To validate our methodology, we introduce and prove the \emph{Bimodal Constraint-Avoidance Utility Theorem}, demonstrating that while feasible negotiations follow classical convex bounds, highly constrained scenarios undergo a phase transition governed by an inverse rational decay envelope. Empirical results generated using a locally hosted 1B-parameter model (\texttt{otel-llm-1b-it}) confirm these dual-regime bounds. Our cognitive de-biasing successfully dismantles rigid negotiation patterns, forcing agents into active exploration to safely ride SLA boundaries and boost system energy savings up to 25\%. Crucially, the lightweight 1B LLM achieves sub-second inference latencies (0.95s mean), ensuring our multi-agent framework is compatible with the operational timescales of the O-RAN non-Real-Time RAN Intelligent Controller (non-RT RIC)\footnote{Our source code is available for non-commercial use at https://github.com/HatimChergui.

</details>


### [140] [Robustness Analysis of Australia's Internet Using a Multilayer Network Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18737)
*Benjamin Lang,Matthew Roughan,Mengbin Ye*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该研究通过构建澳大利亚六家主要ISP的多层网络模型，分析了独立提供商网络之间的互联如何为单一网络故障提供冗余，以及共享风险链路组（SRLGs）带来的潜在脆弱性。结果表明，网络多样性能够提供冗余，即使在存在共享风险的情况下也依然有效，凸显了多样化网络生态系统的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澳大利亚的互联网依赖于由多个长途链路网络构成的架构。研究人员旨在探究独立提供商网络之间的互联如何为单一网络故障提供冗余，以及由共享物理依赖（如共享管道）导致的共享风险链路组（SRLGs）故障可能带来的潜在脆弱性。

Method: 引入一种广义多层网络模型，其中每一层代表单个互联网服务提供商（ISP）的网络，并设有一个互联网交换中心（IXP）层以促进ISP网络之间的互联。基于此构建了一个包含澳大利亚六家主要ISP的特定模型，并对其进行了故障分析。

Result: 对该网络进行的故障分析显示，多样性能够提供冗余，即使在存在共享风险的情况下也依然有效，表明了多样化网络生态系统的重要性。

Conclusion: 网络多样性是确保互联网弹性和可靠性的关键因素。即使在面临共享风险链路组故障的情况下，多样化的网络生态系统仍能提供有效的冗余，这对于网络规划和风险管理具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Australia depends on an Internet built from multiple networks of long-haul links. We study the interactions of these independent provider networks to investigate how the peering between these networks provides redundancy for failures on a single network, as well as the potential vulnerabilities introduced by failures of Shared Risk Link Groups (SRLGs), whereby ostensibly independent links of different providers fail simultaneously due to joint physical dependencies such as shared conduits. We introduce a generalised multilayer network model in which each layer represents the network of an individual Internet Service Provider (ISP), along with an Internet Exchange Point (IXP) layer that facilitates interconnections between ISP networks. We construct an Australia-specific model, consisting of six major ISPs. A failure analysis is performed on this network, revealing that diversity provides redundancy, even in the presence of shared risks, indicating the importance of a diverse network ecosystem.

</details>


### [141] [5G UE and Network Asset Administration Shells for the Integration of 5G and Industry 4.0 Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18762)
*Jorge Gómez-Jerez,Jorge Cañete-Martín,M. Carmen Lucas-Estañ,Javier Gozalvez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究首次设计并实现了面向5G系统的资产管理壳(AAS)，包含UE AAS和NW AAS，遵循5G-ACIA指南及工业4.0和3GPP标准，旨在促进5G与工业4.0系统的集成并降低网络管理复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G是实现智能制造全面数字化转型的关键技术，但5G网络管理的复杂性阻碍了其与工业4.0系统的有效集成。资产管理局(AAS)作为解决方案，可以简化5G与工业4.0应用和系统的集成过程。

Method: 设计并实现了首个完整的5G系统AAS，包含5G用户设备(UE)AAS和5G网络(NW)AAS两个组件。开发过程严格遵循5G-ACIA指南、Plattform Industrie 4.0标准以及3GPP标准规范，确保与现有工业标准和通信标准的兼容性。

Result: 成功构建了首个公开发布的完整5G系统AAS，能够提供并暴露5G所需的数据和功能接口，为社区提供了标准化的5G系统数字化表示方法。

Conclusion: 该5G系统AAS的实现对促进5G技术在工业4.0环境中的应用具有重要意义，为解决5G网络管理复杂性和系统集成问题提供了标准化途径，有助于加速智能制造的数字化转型进程。

Abstract: 5G is a fundamental technology for the full digitalization of smart manufacturing. The use of Asset Administration Shells (AAS) can facilitate the integration of 5G with Industry 4.0 systems and applications while minimizing the complexities associated with the 5G network management. This study presents the design and implementation of the first full 5G system AAS that is openly released to the community [1]. It includes a 5G UE (User Equipment) AAS and a 5G NW (network) AAS that have been designed following the 5G-ACIA guidelines as well as the Plattform Industrie 4.0 and 3GPP standards. The AASs have been defined to provide and expose the data and capabilities of 5G necessary to facilitate the integration of 5G with Industry 4.0 systems and applications.

</details>


### [142] [An open-source implementation and validation of 5G NR Configured Grant for URLLC in ns-3 5G LENA: a scheduling case study in Industry 4.0 scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18763)
*Ana Larrañaga,M. Carmen Lucas-Estañ,Sandra Lagén,Zoraze Ali,Imanol Martinez,Javier Gozálvez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次在开源的ns-3 5G-LENA系统级仿真器中实现了5G NR配置授权（CG）功能，填补了开源仿真工具不支持CG的空白，并通过工业4.0场景验证了其降低上行传输时延的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工厂数字化转型对通信网络提出了严格的时延和可靠性要求，5G URLLC技术应运而生。然而，动态调度机制因信令交互引入额外时延，配置授权（CG）可解决此问题。但目前缺乏支持CG的开源5G NR仿真工具，无法有效评估网络性能和验证新方案。

Method: 在开源ns-3 5G-LENA系统级仿真器中实现了配置授权（CG）功能，并公开了代码。同时增强了对OFDMA的实现，以更好地反映5G NR的灵活性。通过工业4.0场景进行验证，分析不同调度策略下CG的时延性能。

Result: 仿真结果表明，5G-LENA实现的CG所获得的时延性能与先前分析研究的结果高度吻合。研究强调了高效无线资源利用对降低时延、满足关键工业服务要求的重要性。

Conclusion: 本研究为学术界和工业界提供了首个支持配置授权的开源5G NR仿真工具，使研究人员能够在真实场景中评估URLLC性能，为满足工业制造对超低时延通信的需求提供了重要的实验平台和验证手段。

Abstract: Factories are undergoing a digital transformation towards cost-efficient, zero-defect manufacturing, creating the need for communication networks capable of meeting stringent latency and reliability requirements. 5G and beyond networks are being designed to support Ultra-Reliable Low-Latency Communications (URLLC). However, the use of dynamic scheduling for uplink transmissions introduces additional latency due to the signaling required to request and allocate radio resources. To reduce this latency, 5G NR defines Configured Grant (CG), which pre-allocates uplink resources and eliminates the need for per-packet scheduling requests. Accurate simulation tools implementing URLLC features are essential to evaluate the capability of 5G networks to support time-critical services and to investigate new solutions. Nevertheless, the availability of such tools is limited and, to the best of the authors' knowledge, no open-source 5G NR simulator currently supports CG. To address this gap, this paper presents the first implementation of CG in an open-source 5G NR simulator. Specifically, CG has been integrated into the ns-3 5G-LENA system-level simulator and made publicly available. In addition, the Orthogonal Frequency Division Multiple Access (OFDMA) implementation has been enhanced to better reflect the flexibility of 5G NR. The implementation is validated through Industry 4.0 scenarios, where the latency performance achieved with CG under different scheduling policies is analyzed. Results show that the latency obtained with 5G-LENA closely matches that reported in previous analytical studies. Furthermore, the study highlights the importance of efficient radio resource utilization to reduce latency and satisfy the requirements of critical industrial services.

</details>


### [143] [Direct-V2X Support with 5G Network-based Communications: Performance, Challenges and Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18764)
*M. C. Lucas-Estañ,B. Coll-Perales,T. Shimizu,J. Gozálvez,T. Higuchi,S. Avedisov,O. Altintas,M. Sepulcre*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了基于5G网络的V2N2V通信支持关键V2X服务的可行性，证明通过MEC部署可实现低延迟通信，但需要本地对等点和共享数据中心来解决多运营商场景下的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估5G V2N2V通信在支持关键V2X服务方面的可行性，特别是在单运营商和多运营商场景下满足端到端延迟要求，以补充直接V2V连接或缓解 sidelink 拥塞。

Method: 在单运营商和多运营商场景下，评估不同网络部署方案的5G V2N2V通信端到端延迟性能，重点分析基于MEC的网络部署架构。

Result: 关键V2X服务可通过基于MEC部署的5G V2N2V通信实现支持，但在非对称网络部署场景下需要采用本地对等点、共享数据中心或MEC联邦机制来解决相关挑战。

Conclusion: V2N2V通信具备补充直接V2V连接的潜力，可提高通信可靠性或在sidelink网络拥塞时分流流量，为V2X服务提供更灵活的通信选择。

Abstract: This study analyzes the feasibility of supporting critical V2X services using 5G network-based Vehicle-to-Network-to-Vehicle (V2N2V) communications. The study evaluates the end-to-end latency of 5G V2N2V communications under different network deployments in single and multi-operator scenarios. The study shows that critical V2X services can be supported using 5G V2N2V communications over MEC-based network deployments. However, this requires the use of local peering points and shared data centers or MEC federation to address challenges arising from asymmetric network deployments. This opens the possibility for V2N2V communications to complement direct Vehicle-to-Vehicle (V2V) connections for increased reliability or to offload traffic under sidelink network congestion.

</details>


### [144] [Atomic Handover for 6G Nomadic Non-Public Networks Using Edge-Based Spectrum Brokering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19058)
*Daniel Lindenschmitt,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对游牧非公共网络(NNPN)提出了一种零接触切换方案，通过边缘频谱代理和认知频谱管理器实现网络选择与频谱分配的原子化切换，在救护车移动医疗场景仿真中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在6G系统中，NNPN对于应急响应、临时事件等移动场景至关重要，但在异构基础设施间移动时，网络附着和频谱接入需同时调整，保持无缝连接具有挑战性，现有切换机制无法有效解决这一问题。

Method: 提出零接触切换架构，引入边缘频谱代理结合认知频谱管理器，支持原子化切换过程，将网络选择与频谱分配在单一步骤中协同完成；采用MATLAB仿真搭建移动医疗场景。

Result: 在救护车NNPN在公共陆地移动网络(PLMN)与非公共网络(NPN)之间切换的仿真实验中，验证了所提架构能够有效实现网络选择与频谱分配的联合优化，支持无缝连接。

Conclusion: 所提出的零接触切换方案成功解决了NNPN在异构网络环境中的移动性管理与频谱协调问题，为6G系统中移动可部署网络基础设施提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Nomadic Non-Public Networks (NNPN) are expected to play an important role in future 6G systems by enabling mobile and rapidly deployable network infrastructures for scenarios such as emergency response or temporary events. In such environments, maintaining seamless connectivity is challenging, as both network attachment and spectrum access may need to be adapted simultaneously when moving across heterogeneous infrastructures. In this paper, we investigate handover mechanisms for NNPN and propose a zero-touch approach that jointly considers mobility management and dynamic spectrum coordination. The proposed architecture introduces an edge-based Spectrum Broker in combination with a Cognitive Spectrum Manager to support an atomic handover procedure, where network selection and spectrum allocation are performed in a single step. The concept is evaluated using a MATLAB-based simulation of a mobile healthcare scenario, where an ambulance with its NNPN transitions between Public Land Mobile Networks (PLMN) and Non-Public Networks (NPN).

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Characterizing Opinion Evolution of Networked LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18276)
*Caleb Probine,Yigit Ege Bayiz,Filippos Fotiadis,Samuel Li,Yunhao Yang,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究发现经典意见动力学模型需要引入“偏向性”才能有效建模大语言模型（LLM）多智能体网络中的观点传播，加入偏向性参数后模型误差降低高达88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）越来越多地在多智能体系统中相互交互（如人类话语模拟、影响力操作和全LLM驱动的社交平台），出现了新的观点传播机制。目前学界尚不清楚经典意见动力学模型是否能够捕捉LLM网络的行为特征，因此需要对此进行系统性研究。

Method: 作者将经典意见动力学模型应用于LLM多智能体网络，首先测试了朴素的平均化风格模型，随后对模型进行简单修改。通过对比不同模型配置，重点分析了“偏向性（bias）”——即智能体回归的内在观点——这一参数的作用，并在不同的模型家族、讨论主题和网络结构中进行了验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，朴素的平均化模型无法有效追踪LLM的意见动态。引入偏向性参数后，模型保真度得到显著提升，累计估计的平均意见误差降低了高达88%。该结论在不同的模型家族、讨论主题和网络结构中均表现出良好的泛化性。

Conclusion: 偏向性是驱动LLM意见动态的关键因素，经典意见动力学模型通过简单修改即可有效捕捉LLM网络的行为特征。这一发现为理解和预测LLM多智能体系统中的观点传播提供了新的理论基础和建模工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly interact with one another in multi-agent systems, from simulations of human discourse to influence operations and fully LLM-driven social platforms. These interactions give rise to new regimes of opinion propagation that are not yet well understood. We investigate whether classical opinion dynamics models, which have long been used to explain how interactions shape collective beliefs in human societies, can capture the behavior of LLM networks. We find that, while naive averaging-style models fail to track LLMs' opinion dynamics, simple modifications yield substantial gains in modeling fidelity. In particular, bias, an innate opinion toward which agents regress, emerges as a significant driver of LLM opinion dynamics, with its inclusion reducing cumulative estimated mean opinion error by up to 88%. We additionally find that these conclusions generalize across model families, discussion topics, and networks.

</details>


### [146] [PersonalPlan: Planning Multi-Agent Systems for Personalized Programming Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18633)
*Zhiyuan Wen,Jiannong Cao,Peng Gao,Haochen Shi,Wengpan Kuan,Bo Yuan,Xiuxiu Qi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了MAP-PPL数据集（包含3,043个查询-画像-规划实例）和PersonalPlan两阶段多智能体规划器，通过层次化SFT和奖励自适应GRPO方法，在编程教育中实现了高可执行性、个性化和教学质量的规划生成，8B和32B参数模型即达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统在复杂规划方面表现出色，但现有规划器缺乏用户画像约束和教学支架设计，无法满足编程教育中多样化的学习者背景需求，制约了个性化编程学习的效果。

Method: ①构建MAP-PPL数据集：基于Stack Overflow问题组和学习者画像，包含代理、子任务、可执行步骤和前置依赖关系的完整规划；②提出PersonalPlan两阶段规划器：第一阶段使用独立LoRA适配器进行层次化监督学习，分别处理画像感知的任务分解和步骤依赖规划；第二阶段应用奖励自适应GRPO策略，鼓励模型生成可执行、个性化且具有教学支架的规划。

Result: PersonalPlan在MAP-PPL数据集上显著优于前沿LLM、通用MAS框架和代理规划器；仅使用8B和32B参数变体即达到SOTA水平的规划可执行性、个性化和教学质量，有效协调多智能体系统进行代理-学生交互。

Conclusion: MAP-PPL填补了编程教育领域个性化多智能体规划数据集的空白，PersonalPlan证明了通过画像条件约束和教学支架设计，中小规模参数模型即可在个性化编程教育规划任务上超越大规模通用模型，为编程教育智能化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Effective programming education requires personalized instruction adapted to diverse learner backgrounds. However, while LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) excel at complex planning, existing planners often lack profile-grounding and pedagogical scaffolding, thereby undermining personalized programming learning. To fill in the gap, we first introduce \textbf{MAP-PPL} (\textbf{M}ulti-\textbf{A}gent \textbf{P}lans for \textbf{P}ersonalized \textbf{P}rogramming \textbf{L}earning), a profile-conditioned multi-agent planning dataset with 3{,}043 query--profile--plan instances from 1{,}730 Stack Overflow question groups and 2{,}738 learner profiles. Each plan specifies agents, subtasks, executable steps, and prerequisite dependencies. Then, we propose \textbf{PersonalPlan}, a two-stage MAS planner that first performs hierarchical SFT with separate LoRA adapters for profile-aware task decomposition and step dependency planning, then applies a Reward-Adaptive GRPO to encourage the model to generate executable, personalized, and pedagogically scaffolded plans. Extensive experiments on MAP-PPL comparing PersonalPlan against frontier LLMs, generic MAS frameworks, and agentic planners demonstrate its superiority. With only 8B and 32B variants, PersonalPlan achieves state-of-the-art plan executability, personalization, and pedagogical quality, effectively orchestrating MAS for agent-student interactions.

</details>


### [147] [Gender Bias in LLM Hiring Decisions: Evidence from a Japanese Context and Evaluation of Mitigation Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18649)
*Serena A. Hoffstedde,Machiko Hirota,Akshara Nadayanur Sathis Kanna,Rihito Kotani,Ujwal Kumar,Gabriele Trovato,Phan Xuan Tan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究将LLM招聘性别偏见研究扩展至日本企业语境，发现五种主流LLM均存在显著的亲女性偏见，并验证了消除候选人姓名是有效的偏见缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于LLM招聘性别偏见的研究主要关注英语和西方格式的简历，缺乏对非西方语境（如日本）的考察。本研究旨在探究亲女性偏见是否存在于日本企业招聘场景，并评估两种实际可行的偏见缓解策略的有效性。

Method: 采用反事实简历设计，构建了60份日本标准“履历书”格式简历，选取了12对基于语言学标准的性别信号姓名对，使用五种主流LLM（Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-4o, DeepSeek-V3, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Llama 3.3 70B）。在基线、提示指令和隐私过滤三种条件下进行了43,200次API调用，并使用交叉随机效应线性混合模型进行数据分析。

Result: 所有五个模型均确认存在显著的亲女性偏见，成功在非西方语境下复现了西方研究的结果。提示层面的性别中立指令未能有效减少偏见。姓名依赖分析正式确认候选人姓名是主要的性别信号渠道：从提示中移除姓名可将女性效应减少近全部幅度。隐私过滤器与GPT-4o的内容安全过滤器之间存在意外的兼容性问题，导致42%的拒绝率。

Conclusion: 研究证实LLM在招聘决策中的亲女性偏见具有跨文化普遍性，存在于日本企业语境。提示工程方法（性别中立指令）难以消除偏见，而姓名匿名化（移除姓名）是更有效的缓解策略，但在实际部署中可能面临与模型安全机制的兼容性挑战。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in hiring workflows, yet most research on gender bias in LLM hiring decisions has focused on English-language, Western-format resumes. This study examines whether pro-female gender bias extends to a Japanese corporate context and evaluates two practical mitigation strategies. Using a counterfactual resume design with 60 Japanese rirekisho-format resumes, 12 name pairs selected on linguistically grounded gender-signal criteria, and five state-of-the-art LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-4o, DeepSeek-V3, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Llama 3.3 70B), we conducted 43,200 API calls across baseline, prompt instruction, and privacy filter conditions. A crossed random-effects linear mixed model confirms a significant pro-female bias across all five models, replicating Western findings in a non-Western context. A prompt-level gender-neutrality instruction produces no meaningful reduction in bias. A name-reliance analysis formally identifies the candidate name as the primary gender channel: removing the name from the prompt reduces the female effect by nearly its full magnitude. An unexpected incompatibility between the privacy filter and GPT-4o's content safety filter, resulting in a 42% refusal rate, highlights a practical deployment challenge for name anonymization in LLM-assisted recruitment pipelines.

</details>


### [148] [EARS: Explanatory Abstention for Reliable Sub-Agent Modeling in Large-scale Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18668)
*Shuang Xie,Yunan Lu,Han Li,Lingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出EARS框架，通过让子智能体在遇到无法处理的请求时提供可解释的弃权反馈，将大型企业多智能体系统的整体响应通过率从68.5%提升至78.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在企业级集中式多智能体系统中，基于小型微调模型的子智能体在面对模糊、未明确指定、错误路由或不支持的请求时，往往过度回答并产生幻觉输出，而非提供可操作的反馈。这种能力约束校准的缺失严重影响了系统的可靠性。

Method: 提出EARS框架，将子智能体弃权重构为智能体间通信协议。利用校准后的LLM-as-a-Judge模型集合构建人机交互数据，生成结构化的弃权标签和基于失败模式分类法的理由说明。基于这些数据微调子智能体，使其能够检测失败条件并向协调器返回理由，以便进行澄清、重新路由或回退操作。

Result: 在支持企业商业智能工作流的大规模生产电商助手中进行评估，EARS将整体响应通过率从68.5%提升至78.9%。

Conclusion: 子智能体侧的可解释弃权机制能够有效提升多智能体系统的可靠性，为企业级应用提供了一种可行的生产导向解决方案。

Abstract: In large-scale enterprise settings, centralized multi-agent systems (MAS) are increasingly adopted, in which a coordinator delegates user requests to lightweight, domain-specialized sub-agents.
